32248:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32302:   (function installModalResilience(){
32303:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32304:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32305:       if(!ac)return;
32306:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32307:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32308:       e.preventDefault();
32309:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32310:     },true);
32311:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32312:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32313:       if(!root)return;
32314:       e.preventDefault();
32315:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32316:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32317:     });
32318:     try{
32319:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32320:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32321:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32322:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32323:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32324:       });
32325:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32326:     }catch(e){}
32327:   }());

32183:   function opClear(k){if(k)delete H.ops[k]}
32184:   async function actor(){var a=await supabase.auth.getUser();if(a.error||!a.data.user)throw Error('جلسة المستخدم غير صالحة');var u=await supabase.from('users').select('id,email,company_id,role,name,status,phone,employee_id,default_branch_id,active_warehouse_role').eq('auth_id',a.data.user.id).maybeSingle();if(u.error)throw u.error;if(!u.data||!u.data.id||!u.data.company_id)throw Error('تعذر تحديد سياق الموظف والشركة');H.actor=u.data;H.companyId=u.data.company_id}
32185:   async function q(view,payload){var r=await supabase.rpc('hr_query',{p_view:view,p_payload:payload||{}});if(r.error)throw r.error;if(!r.data||r.data.success===false)throw Error((r.data&&(r.data.msg||r.data.code))||'فشل قراءة HR');return r.data}

32190:   function stat(title,value,icon,cls){return '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-5 shadow-sm"><div class="flex items-center justify-between"><div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500 font-bold">'+esc(title)+'</div><div class="text-2xl font-black mt-2">'+esc(value)+'</div></div><div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center '+(cls||'bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700')+'"><i class="fas '+icon+'"></i></div></div></div>'}

32235: function closeModal(){var r=E('rw-hr-modal-root');if(r)r.remove()}
32236:   function ppl(){return H.employees.filter(function(e){return String(e.role||'').toLowerCase()!=='owner'&&e.role!=='مالك'}).map(function(e){return{value:e.id,label:(e.name||e.email)+' — '+e.email}})}
32237:   async function loadPeople(){var d=await q('employees');H.employees=d.rows||[];return H.employees}

32243:   function scheduleOpts(rows){return (rows||[]).map(function(x){return{value:x.id,label:x.name}})}

32248:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32261:   async function disburseAdvance(id){
32262:     var tr=await supabase.from('treasury').select('id,account_name,account_code,type,current_balance,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).eq('is_active',true).in('type',['Cash','Bank']).order('account_name');
32263:     if(tr.error)throw tr.error;
32264:     var rows=tr.data||[];
32265:     if(!rows.length){toast('لا توجد خزينة أو حساب بنكي فعال للصرف','error');return;}
32266:     var opts=rows.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.id)+'">'+esc(x.account_name||x.account_code)+' · '+esc(x.type)+' · '+money(x.current_balance)+'</option>';}).join('');
32267:     var r=await Swal.fire({title:'صرف السلفة عبر سند Payments',html:'<div class="text-right text-sm text-slate-600 mb-2">سيتم إنشاء سند صرف مركزي وقيد محاسبي وربطه بالسلفة والموظف.</div><select id="hr-advance-treasury" class="swal2-select w-full">'+opts+'</select>',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'إنشاء سند الصرف',cancelButtonText:'إلغاء',preConfirm:function(){var t=E('hr-advance-treasury');if(!t||!t.value){Swal.showValidationMessage('اختر خزينة الصرف');return false;}return t.value;}});
32268:     if(!r.isConfirmed)return;
32269:     await c('advance.disburse',{advance_id:id,treasury_id:r.value},'disburse-advance:'+id);
32270:     toast('تم الصرف من خلال سند Payments مركزي وربطه بالسلفة','success');
32271:     return render();
32272:   }

32311:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32312:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');

32314:       e.preventDefault();

32316:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32317:     });
32318:     try{
32319:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32320:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32321:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32322:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32323:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32324:       });
32325:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32326:     }catch(e){}
32327:   }());

29744: 
29745: function _exportReportCsv() {
29746:     var resultDiv = byId('report-result');
29747: 
29748:     if (!resultDiv) {
29749:         _showToast('لا يوجد تقرير للتصدير', 'info');
29750:         return;
29751:     }
29752: 
29753:     var table = resultDiv.querySelector('table');
29754: 
29755:     if (!table) {
29756:         _showToast('لا يوجد جدول قابل للتصدير', 'info');
29757:         return;
29758:     }
29759: 
29760:     var rows = table.querySelectorAll('tr');
29761:     var csv = [];
29762: 
29763:     function csvCell(value) {
29764:         var s = String(value == null ? '' : value)
29765:             .replace(/\r?\n|\r/g, ' ')
29766:             .replace(/"/g, '""');
29767: 
29768:         return '"' + s + '"';
29769:     }
29770: 
29771:     for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
29772:         var cells = rows[i].querySelectorAll('th,td');
29773:         var line = [];
29774: 
29775:         for (var j = 0; j < cells.length; j++) {
29776:             line.push(csvCell(cells[j].innerText || ''));
29777:         }
29778: 
29779:         csv.push(line.join(','));

32305:       if(!ac)return;
32306:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32307:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;

32312:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32313:       if(!root)return;
32314:       e.preventDefault();

32316:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32317:     });
32318:     try{
32319:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32320:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32321:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32322:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32323:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32324:       });
32325:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32326:     }catch(e){}
32327:   }());

32301:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
32302:   (function installModalResilience(){
32303:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32304:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32305:       if(!ac)return;
32306:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32307:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32308:       e.preventDefault();
32309:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32310:     },true);
32311:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32312:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32313:       if(!root)return;
32314:       e.preventDefault();
32315:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32316:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32317:     });
32318:     try{
32319:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32320:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32321:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32322:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32323:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32324:       });
32325:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32326:     }catch(e){}
32327:   }());

32332: // ============================================================
32333: // RW_CRM – إدارة علاقات العملاء (CRM)
32334: // ============================================================
32335: var RW_CRM = (function() {
32336:     'use strict';
32337: 
32338:     var state = {
32339:         customers: [],
32340:         assignees: [],
32341:         kpi: {},
32342:         search: '',
32343:         activeOnly: false,
32344:         searchTimer: null
32345:     };
32346: 
32347:     function _esc(s) {
32348:         return String(s == null ? '' : s)
32349:             .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
32350:             .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
32351:             .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
32352:             .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
32353:             .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
32354:     }
32355: 
32356:     function _fmtNum(n) {
32357:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG');
32358:     }
32359: 
32360:     function _fmtMoney(n) {
32361:         return Number(n || 0).toLocaleString('ar-EG') + ' EGP';
32362:     }
32363: 
32364:     function _today() {
32365:         var d = new Date();
32366:         var local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
32367:         return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
32368:     }
32369: 
32370:     function _statusLabel(s) {
32371:         var map = {
32372:             Open: 'مفتوحة',
32373:             'معلقة': 'معلقة',
32374:             completed: 'مكتملة',
32375:             'مكتملة': 'مكتملة',
32376:             cancelled: 'ملغاة',
32377:             'ملغاة': 'ملغاة'
32378:         };
32379:         return map[s] || s || 'غير محددة';
32380:     }
32381: 
32382:     function _statusClass(s) {
32383:         if (s === 'completed' || s === 'مكتملة') return 'bg-green-100 text-green-700';
32384:         if (s === 'cancelled' || s === 'ملغاة') return 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600';
32385:         return 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700';
32386:     }
32387: 
32388:     function _assignedLabel(customer) {
32389:         var rows = Array.isArray(customer && customer.assigned_to) ? customer.assigned_to : [];
32390:         if (!rows.length) return 'غير مسند';
32391:         var active = rows.filter(function(x) { return x && x.active !== false; });
32392:         if (!active.length) active = rows;
32393:         return active.slice(0, 2).map(function(x) {
32394:             return x.name || x.email || '—';
32395:         }).join('، ') + (active.length > 2 ? ' +' + (active.length - 2) : '');
32396:     }

32398:     async function _loadDirectory() {
32399:         var res = await supabase.rpc('crm_customer_directory', {
32400:             p_search: state.search || null,
32401:             p_active_only: state.activeOnly,
32402:             p_limit: 200,
32403:             p_offset: 0
32404:         });
32405:         if (res.error) throw res.error;
32406: 
32407:         var payload = res.data || {};
32408:         state.customers = Array.isArray(payload.customers) ? payload.customers : [];
32409:         state.assignees = Array.isArray(payload.assignees) ? payload.assignees : [];
32410:         state.kpi = payload.kpi || {};
32411:         return payload;
32412:     }
32413: 
32414:     function _renderKpis() {
32415:         var k = state.kpi || {};
32416:         return '<div class="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">' +
32417:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">إجمالي العملاء</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.total_customers) + '</div></div>' +
32418:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">عملاء نشطون</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-green-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.active_customers) + '</div></div>' +
32419:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">ذمم مسجلة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-red-600 mt-2">' + _fmtMoney(k.master_debt_total) + '</div></div>' +
32420:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متابعات مفتوحة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-amber-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.open_followups) + '</div></div>' +
32421:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">متأخرة</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.overdue_followups) + '</div></div>' +
32422:             '<div class="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-4"><div class="text-xs text-gray-500">مستحقة اليوم</div><div class="text-2xl font-black text-blue-600 mt-2">' + _fmtNum(k.due_today) + '</div></div>' +
32423:         '</div>';
32424:     }
32425: 
32426:     function _renderTable() {
32427:         if (!state.customers.length) {
32428:             return '<div class="text-center py-16 text-gray-400"><div class="text-5xl mb-3">👥</div><div class="font-black text-lg">لا توجد عملاء مطابقون</div><div class="text-sm mt-2">غيّر البحث أو الفلاتر ثم أعد المحاولة.</div></div>';
32429:         }
32430: 
32431:         var html = '<div class="overflow-x-auto"><table class="w-full text-sm">' +
32432:             '<thead class="bg-slate-50"><tr>' +
32433:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">العميل</th>' +
32434:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التواصل</th>' +
32435:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">التصنيف</th>' +
32436:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المبيعات</th>' +
32437:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الأوردرات</th>' +
32438:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">المتابعة القادمة</th>' +
32439:             '<th class="p-3 text-right">المسؤول</th>' +
32440:             '<th class="p-3 text-center">الإجراء</th>' +
32441:             '</tr></thead><tbody>';
32442: 
32443:         for (var i = 0; i < state.customers.length; i++) {
32444:             var c = state.customers[i] || {};
32445:             var overdue = Number(c.overdue_followups || 0) > 0;
32446:             var next = c.next_followup_date ? String(c.next_followup_date) : '—';
32447: 
32448:             html += '<tr class="border-b hover:bg-slate-50">' +
32449:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-black">' + _esc(c.name) + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.customer_code) + '</div></td>' +
32450:                 '<td class="p-3"><div>' + _esc(c.phone || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.area || '—') + '</div></td>' +
32451:                 '<td class="p-3"><div class="font-bold">' + _esc(c.customer_type || '—') + '</div><div class="text-xs text-gray-400">' + _esc(c.payment_type || '—') + '</div></td>' +
32452:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtMoney(c.sales_total) + '</td>' +
32453:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center font-black">' + _fmtNum(c.order_count) + '</td>' +
32454:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-black ' + (overdue ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700') + '">' + _esc(next) + '</span></td>' +
32455:                 '<td class="p-3">' + _esc(_assignedLabel(c)) + '</td>' +
32456:                 '<td class="p-3 text-center"><button type="button" data-crm-open360="' + _esc(c.id) + '" class="px-4 py-2 bg-indigo-600 text-white rounded-xl font-black">متابعة</button></td>' +
32457:             '</tr>';
32458:         }
32459: 
32460:         return html + '</tbody></table></div>';
32461:     }
32462: 
32463:     function _bindDirectory() {
32464:         var search = byId('crm-search');
32465:         if (search) {
32466:             search.value = state.search;
32467:             search.addEventListener('input', function() {
32468:                 state.search = search.value.trim();
32469:                 clearTimeout(state.searchTimer);
32470:                 state.searchTimer = setTimeout(function() {
32471:                     _loadDirectory().then(function() {
32472:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-customers-list'), _renderTable());
32473:                         _bindOpen360();
32474:                         safeHTML(byId('crm-kpis'), _renderKpis());
32475:                     }).catch(function(e) {
32476:                         showToast(e.message || 'فشل البحث', 'error');
32477:                     });
32478:                 }, 250);
32479:             });
32480:         }

32248:   function buildTree(ds){var by={},root=[];(ds||[]).forEach(function(x){by[x.id]={id:x.id,name:x.name,code:x.code,parent:x.parent_department_id,manager:x.manager_employee_id,children:[]}});Object.keys(by).forEach(function(k){var x=by[k];if(x.parent&&by[x.parent])by[x.parent].children.push(x);else root.push(x)});function node(x,depth){var manager=H.employees.filter(function(e){return e.id===x.manager})[0];return '<div class="mr-'+Math.min(depth*3,12)+' rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 p-4 bg-white shadow-sm"><div class="flex justify-between gap-3"><div><div class="font-black">'+esc(x.name)+'</div><div class="text-xs text-slate-500">'+esc(x.code||'-')+(manager?' · مدير: '+esc(manager.name):'')+'</div></div>'+badge(x.children.length+' فرعي','info')+'</div>'+(x.children.length?'<div class="mt-3 space-y-3 border-r-2 border-slate-100 pr-4">'+x.children.map(function(c){return node(c,depth+1)}).join('')+'</div>':'')+'</div>'}return root.map(function(x){return node(x,0)}).join('')||'<div class="py-10 text-center text-slate-400 font-bold">لم تُنشأ إدارات بعد</div>'}

32261:   async function disburseAdvance(id){
32262:     var tr=await supabase.from('treasury').select('id,account_name,account_code,type,current_balance,is_active').eq('company_id',H.companyId).eq('is_active',true).in('type',['Cash','Bank']).order('account_name');
32263:     if(tr.error)throw tr.error;
32264:     var rows=tr.data||[];
32265:     if(!rows.length){toast('لا توجد خزينة أو حساب بنكي فعال للصرف','error');return;}
32266:     var opts=rows.map(function(x){return '<option value="'+esc(x.id)+'">'+esc(x.account_name||x.account_code)+' · '+esc(x.type)+' · '+money(x.current_balance)+'</option>';}).join('');
32267:     var r=await Swal.fire({title:'صرف السلفة عبر سند Payments',html:'<div class="text-right text-sm text-slate-600 mb-2">سيتم إنشاء سند صرف مركزي وقيد محاسبي وربطه بالسلفة والموظف.</div><select id="hr-advance-treasury" class="swal2-select w-full">'+opts+'</select>',showCancelButton:true,confirmButtonText:'إنشاء سند الصرف',cancelButtonText:'إلغاء',preConfirm:function(){var t=E('hr-advance-treasury');if(!t||!t.value){Swal.showValidationMessage('اختر خزينة الصرف');return false;}return t.value;}});
32268:     if(!r.isConfirmed)return;
32269:     await c('advance.disburse',{advance_id:id,treasury_id:r.value},'disburse-advance:'+id);
32270:     toast('تم الصرف من خلال سند Payments مركزي وربطه بالسلفة','success');
32271:     return render();
32272:   }

32300:   function realtime(){try{if(H.channel)supabase.removeChannel(H.channel);var refresh=function(){clearTimeout(H.timer);H.timer=setTimeout(function(){render()},700)};var tables=['employee_profiles','employee_attendance','employee_leave_requests','employee_documents','hr_departments','hr_positions','hr_employee_assignments','hr_employee_schedule_assignments','hr_work_schedules','hr_attendance_events','hr_work_entries','hr_leave_types','hr_leave_balances','hr_requests','hr_request_approvals','hr_salary_advances','hr_salary_components','hr_contracts','hr_contract_components','hr_payroll_periods','hr_payroll_runs','hr_payslips','hr_payslip_lines','hr_payroll_accounting_map'];H.channel=supabase.channel('rw-hr-mother-final');tables.forEach(function(t){H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:t},refresh)});H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:'journal_lines',filter:'party_type=eq.Employee'},refresh);H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'*',schema:'public',table:'cash_box',filter:'party_type=eq.Employee'},refresh);H.channel.on('postgres_changes',{event:'UPDATE',schema:'public',table:'journal_entries',filter:'status=eq.Posted'},refresh);H.channel.subscribe()}catch(e){console.warn('RW_HR realtime',e)}}
32301:   // Resilience layer: modal actions work outside the page-container, async form errors become visible, and 360 is truly read-only.
32302:   (function installModalResilience(){
32303:     document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
32304:       var ac=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('[data-hr-action]');
32305:       if(!ac)return;
32306:       var page=E('rw-page-container');
32307:       if(page&&page.contains(ac))return;
32308:       e.preventDefault();
32309:       handle(ac.getAttribute('data-hr-action'));
32310:     },true);
32311:     window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',function(e){
32312:       var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32313:       if(!root)return;
32314:       e.preventDefault();
32315:       var msg=e.reason&&(e.reason.message||String(e.reason));
32316:       if(msg)toast(msg,'error');
32317:     });
32318:     try{
32319:       var mo=new MutationObserver(function(){
32320:         var root=E('rw-hr-modal-root');
32321:         if(!root||!E('hr360'))return;
32322:         var f=E('rw-hr-form');
32323:         if(f&&f.lastElementChild)f.lastElementChild.style.display='none';
32324:       });
32325:       mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
32326:     }catch(e){}
32327:   }());
32328: 
32329: realtime(); return { render: render, reload: render, openEmployee360: open360 }; }()); window.RW_HR = RW_HR;
